    #[Assert\Callback]
    public function validateOpenApiConstraints(ExecutionContextInterface $context): void
    {
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $violation = $context->buildViolation(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            $path = self::openApiViolationPath($error);
            if ($path !== null) {
                $violation->atPath($path);
            }
            $violation->addViolation();
        }
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload — machinery for the generated validation, not an API to
     * call. It is public only because an enclosing DTO is a different class and needs this view of
     * a nested one. For output, use the Symfony serializer.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP as $phpName => $openApiName) {
            // A required property always exists. An optional one belongs in the payload only when
            // it was actually provided — its presence flag says so, which is also what keeps the
            // flags themselves out of the payload (iterating the object's properties would put
            // them in and trip `additionalProperties: false`).
            $flag = self::OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS[$phpName] ?? null;
            if ($flag !== null && $this->{$flag} !== true) {
                continue;
            }
            $payload[$openApiName] = $this->{$phpName};
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth > 0) {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        }
        try {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        } catch (\OverflowException $error) {
            return [$error->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        // An incomplete check is not a branch mismatch: not/if/anyOf must not invert or swallow it.
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            throw new \OverflowException($path . ' exceeds maximum validation depth.');
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiCallbackValue($value, $schema);
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($propertyValue)) {
                        $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiCallbackValue(mixed $value, array $schema): mixed
    {
        if (!$value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            return $value;
        }

        $format = $schema['format'] ?? null;
        if (!is_string($format)) {
            return $value->format(DateTimeInterface::ATOM);
        }

        return match ($format) {
            'date' => $value->format('Y-m-d'),
            default => $value->format(DateTimeInterface::ATOM),
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return get_object_vars($value);
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    /**
     * The property a message is about, as a value path, or null when it is about the payload itself.
     *
     * The interpreter bakes the subject INTO the sentence — `field "tags" must …`, and one level down
     * `field "tags".id must …` — because the wording has to match the runtime validator's word for
     * word. Reading it back here is what lets the violation carry the FIELD as well as the sentence:
     * an error response groups by property, and every keyword this interpreter owns was landing at
     * the root instead, where a per-field renderer never shows it.
     */
    private static function openApiViolationPath(string $error): ?string
    {
        if (!str_starts_with($error, 'field "')) {
            return null;
        }

        $closing = strpos($error, '"', 7);
        if ($closing === false) {
            return null;
        }

        $property = substr($error, 7, $closing - 7);
        if ($property === '') {
            return null;
        }

        // Anything after the closing quote up to the first space is the rest of the path, so
        // `field "tags".id must …` answers `tags.id` rather than just `tags`.
        $rest = substr($error, $closing + 1);
        $deeper = '';
        if (str_starts_with($rest, '.')) {
            $space = strpos($rest, ' ');
            $deeper = $space === false ? $rest : substr($rest, 0, $space);
        }

        return $property . $deeper;
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }
}

    #[Ignore]
    public function is名前Provided(): bool
    {
        return $this->名前Provided;
    }

    public function setForbiddenKey(mixed $forbiddenKey): void

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this key — true even when its value was null. Set by the setter,
     * so it answers for a request the serializer built and for a DTO assembled by hand alike.

    public function isKeyedProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->keyedProvided;

    #[Assert\Callback]
    public function validateOpenApiConstraints(ExecutionContextInterface $context): void
    {
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $violation = $context->buildViolation(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            $path = self::openApiViolationPath($error);
            if ($path !== null) {
                $violation->atPath($path);
            }
            $violation->addViolation();
        }
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload — machinery for the generated validation, not an API to
     * call. It is public only because an enclosing DTO is a different class and needs this view of
     * a nested one. For output, use the Symfony serializer.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP as $phpName => $openApiName) {
            // A required property always exists. An optional one belongs in the payload only when
            // it was actually provided — its presence flag says so, which is also what keeps the
            // flags themselves out of the payload (iterating the object's properties would put
            // them in and trip `additionalProperties: false`).
            $flag = self::OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS[$phpName] ?? null;
            if ($flag !== null && $this->{$flag} !== true) {
                continue;
            }
            $payload[$openApiName] = $this->{$phpName};
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth > 0) {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        }
        try {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        } catch (\OverflowException $error) {
            return [$error->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        // An incomplete check is not a branch mismatch: not/if/anyOf must not invert or swallow it.
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            throw new \OverflowException($path . ' exceeds maximum validation depth.');
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = $this->toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['dependentSchemas'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['dependentSchemas'] as $propertyName => $depSchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($depSchema) || !array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [...$errors, ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue, $depSchema, $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['not'] ?? null) && $this->matchesOpenApiNode($value, $schema['not'], $depth + 1)) {
            $errors[] = sprintf('%s must not match the \'not\' schema', $path);
        }

        if (is_array($schema['if'] ?? null)) {
            if ($this->matchesOpenApiNode($value, $schema['if'], $depth + 1)) {
                if (is_array($schema['then'] ?? null)) {
                    $errors = [...$errors, ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['then'], $path, $depth + 1)];
                }
            } elseif (is_array($schema['else'] ?? null)) {
                $errors = [...$errors, ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['else'], $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);
        $evaluatedItemIndices = [];
        $prefixCount = 0;

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['prefixItems'] ?? null)) {
            $prefixCount = count($schema['prefixItems']);
            foreach ($schema['prefixItems'] as $index => $itemSchema) {
                if (!is_array($itemSchema) || !array_key_exists($index, $value)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $evaluatedItemIndices[$index] = true;
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($value[$index])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($value[$index], $itemSchema, $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                if ($index < $prefixCount) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($index < $prefixCount) {
                    continue;
                }
                $evaluatedItemIndices[$index] = true;
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['contains'] ?? null)) {
            $matchCount = 0;
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->matchesOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['contains'], $depth + 1)) {
                    $matchCount++;
                    $evaluatedItemIndices[$index] = true;
                }
            }

            $minContains = 1;
            if ($matchCount < $minContains) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain at least %d item(s) matching the \'contains\' schema', $path, $minContains);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && array_key_exists('unevaluatedItems', $schema)) {
            $unevaluatedItemsSchema = $schema['unevaluatedItems'];
            $evaluatedItemIndices += $this->collectOpenApiApplicatorItems($value, $schema, $depth);
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if (array_key_exists($index, $evaluatedItemIndices)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if ($unevaluatedItemsSchema === false) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an unevaluated item at index %s which is not allowed', $path, $index);

                    continue;
                }

                if (!is_array($unevaluatedItemsSchema)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $unevaluatedItemsSchema, $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return $this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return get_object_vars($value);
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    /**
     * The property a message is about, as a value path, or null when it is about the payload itself.
     *
     * The interpreter bakes the subject INTO the sentence — `field "tags" must …`, and one level down
     * `field "tags".id must …` — because the wording has to match the runtime validator's word for
     * word. Reading it back here is what lets the violation carry the FIELD as well as the sentence:
     * an error response groups by property, and every keyword this interpreter owns was landing at
     * the root instead, where a per-field renderer never shows it.
     */
    private static function openApiViolationPath(string $error): ?string
    {
        if (!str_starts_with($error, 'field "')) {
            return null;
        }

        $closing = strpos($error, '"', 7);
        if ($closing === false) {
            return null;
        }

        $property = substr($error, 7, $closing - 7);
        if ($property === '') {
            return null;
        }

        // Anything after the closing quote up to the first space is the rest of the path, so
        // `field "tags".id must …` answers `tags.id` rather than just `tags`.
        $rest = substr($error, $closing + 1);
        $deeper = '';
        if (str_starts_with($rest, '.')) {
            $space = strpos($rest, ' ');
            $deeper = $space === false ? $rest : substr($rest, 0, $space);
        }

        return $property . $deeper;
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, array<string, mixed>>
     */
    private function openApiAppliedBranches(mixed $value, mixed $object, array $schema, int $depth): array
    {
        $branches = [];
        foreach (is_array($schema['allOf'] ?? null) ? $schema['allOf'] : [] as $branch) {
            if (is_array($branch)) {
                $branches[] = $branch;
            }
        }
        foreach (['anyOf', 'oneOf'] as $unionKey) {
            foreach (is_array($schema[$unionKey] ?? null) ? $schema[$unionKey] : [] as $branch) {
                if (is_array($branch) && $this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, '', $depth + 1) === []) {
                    $branches[] = $branch;
                }
            }
        }
        if (is_array($schema['if'] ?? null)) {
            if ($this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['if'], '', $depth + 1) === []) {
                $branches[] = $schema['if'];
                if (is_array($schema['then'] ?? null)) {
                    $branches[] = $schema['then'];
                }
            } elseif (is_array($schema['else'] ?? null)) {
                $branches[] = $schema['else'];
            }
        }
        if (is_array($object) && is_array($schema['dependentSchemas'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['dependentSchemas'] as $trigger => $branch) {
                if (is_string($trigger) && array_key_exists($trigger, $object) && is_array($branch)) {
                    $branches[] = $branch;
                }
            }
        }

        return $branches;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, mixed> $value
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, true>
     */
    private function collectOpenApiApplicatorItems(array $value, array $schema, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }
        $evaluated = [];
        foreach ($this->openApiAppliedBranches($value, null, $schema, $depth) as $branch) {
            $prefixCount = is_array($branch['prefixItems'] ?? null) ? count($branch['prefixItems']) : 0;
            $coversAll = array_key_exists('unevaluatedItems', $branch) && $branch['unevaluatedItems'] !== false;
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if (
                    $coversAll
                    || $index < $prefixCount
                    || (is_array($branch['items'] ?? null) && $index >= $prefixCount)
                    || (is_array($branch['contains'] ?? null) && $this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $branch['contains'], '', $depth + 1) === [])
                ) {
                    $evaluated[$index] = true;
                }
            }
            $evaluated += $this->collectOpenApiApplicatorItems($value, $branch, $depth + 1);
        }

        return $evaluated;
    }
}

===== ApplicatorRefsConditional.php =====
<?php

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Attribute\Context;
use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Attribute\Groups;
use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Attribute\Ignore;
use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Normalizer\AbstractObjectNormalizer;
use Symfony\Component\Validator\Constraints as Assert;
use Symfony\Component\Validator\Context\ExecutionContextInterface;

use function array_key_exists;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function mb_strlen;
use function method_exists;
use function sprintf;

use function str_starts_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * From: ApplicatorRefs.conditional
 *
 * This document uses readOnly/writeOnly, which Symfony can only honour through serialization
 * groups — so every property here carries one. Pass the group, or a writeOnly field ends up in
 * the response:
 *   $serializer->normalize($dto, null, ['groups' => 'read']);
 *   $serializer->denormalize($payload, self::class, null, ['groups' => 'write']);
 */
final class ApplicatorRefsConditional
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'if' => [
            'type' => 'object',
            'required' => [
                'label',
            ],
            'properties' => [
                'label' => [
                    'type' => 'string',
                    'minLength' => 2,
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'then' => [
            'required' => [
                'label',
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP = [
        'label' => 'label',
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS = [
        'label' => 'labelProvided',
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    #[Groups(['read', 'write'])]
    #[Context(normalizationContext: [AbstractObjectNormalizer::SKIP_NULL_VALUES => true])]
    private ?string $label = null;

    #[Ignore]
    private bool $labelProvided = false;

    public function __construct()
    {
    }

    public function getLabel(): ?string
    {
        return $this->label;
    }

    public function setLabel(?string $label): void
    {
        $this->label = $label;
        $this->labelProvided = true;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this key — true even when its value was null. Set by the setter,
     * so it answers for a request the serializer built and for a DTO assembled by hand alike.
     */
    #[Ignore]
    public function isLabelProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->labelProvided;
    }

    #[Assert\Callback]
    public function validateOpenApiConstraints(ExecutionContextInterface $context): void
    {
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $violation = $context->buildViolation(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            $path = self::openApiViolationPath($error);
            if ($path !== null) {
                $violation->atPath($path);
            }
            $violation->addViolation();
        }
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload — machinery for the generated validation, not an API to
     * call. It is public only because an enclosing DTO is a different class and needs this view of
     * a nested one. For output, use the Symfony serializer.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP as $phpName => $openApiName) {
            // A required property always exists. An optional one belongs in the payload only when
            // it was actually provided — its presence flag says so, which is also what keeps the
            // flags themselves out of the payload (iterating the object's properties would put
            // them in and trip `additionalProperties: false`).
            $flag = self::OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS[$phpName] ?? null;
            if ($flag !== null && $this->{$flag} !== true) {
                continue;
            }
            $payload[$openApiName] = $this->{$phpName};
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth > 0) {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        }
        try {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        } catch (\OverflowException $error) {
            return [$error->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        // An incomplete check is not a branch mismatch: not/if/anyOf must not invert or swallow it.
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            throw new \OverflowException($path . ' exceeds maximum validation depth.');
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = $this->toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['if'] ?? null)) {
            if ($this->matchesOpenApiNode($value, $schema['if'], $depth + 1)) {
                if (is_array($schema['then'] ?? null)) {
                    $errors = [...$errors, ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['then'], $path, $depth + 1)];
                }
            } elseif (is_array($schema['else'] ?? null)) {
                $errors = [...$errors, ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['else'], $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return $this->validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return get_object_vars($value);
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    /**
     * The property a message is about, as a value path, or null when it is about the payload itself.
     *
     * The interpreter bakes the subject INTO the sentence — `field "tags" must …`, and one level down
     * `field "tags".id must …` — because the wording has to match the runtime validator's word for
     * word. Reading it back here is what lets the violation carry the FIELD as well as the sentence:
     * an error response groups by property, and every keyword this interpreter owns was landing at
     * the root instead, where a per-field renderer never shows it.
     */
    private static function openApiViolationPath(string $error): ?string
    {
        if (!str_starts_with($error, 'field "')) {
            return null;
        }

        $closing = strpos($error, '"', 7);
        if ($closing === false) {
            return null;
        }

        $property = substr($error, 7, $closing - 7);
        if ($property === '') {
            return null;
        }

        // Anything after the closing quote up to the first space is the rest of the path, so
        // `field "tags".id must …` answers `tags.id` rather than just `tags`.
        $rest = substr($error, $closing + 1);
        $deeper = '';
        if (str_starts_with($rest, '.')) {
            $space = strpos($rest, ' ');
            $deeper = $space === false ? $rest : substr($rest, 0, $space);
        }

        return $property . $deeper;
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }
}

        'trigger' => 'triggerProvided',

    public function validateOpenApiConstraints(ExecutionContextInterface $context): void
    {

    {

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP as $phpName => $openApiName) {
            // A required property always exists. An optional one belongs in the payload only when
            // it was actually provided — its presence flag says so, which is also what keeps the
            // flags themselves out of the payload (iterating the object's properties would put
            // them in and trip `additionalProperties: false`).
            $flag = self::OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS[$phpName] ?? null;
            if ($flag !== null && $this->{$flag} !== true) {
                continue;
            }
            $payload[$openApiName] = $this->{$phpName};
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth > 0) {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        }
        try {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        } catch (\OverflowException $error) {
            return [$error->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        // An incomplete check is not a branch mismatch: not/if/anyOf must not invert or swallow it.
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            throw new \OverflowException($path . ' exceeds maximum validation depth.');
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;

                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;

                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['dependentSchemas'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['dependentSchemas'] as $propertyName => $depSchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($depSchema) || !array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [...$errors, ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue, $depSchema, $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

            return true;

        return match ($type) {

            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),

===== ContainerAliases.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Attribute\Context;
use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Attribute\Groups;
use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Attribute\Ignore;
use Symfony\Component\Serializer\Normalizer\AbstractObjectNormalizer;
use Symfony\Component\Validator\Constraints as Assert;
use Symfony\Component\Validator\Context\ExecutionContextInterface;

use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_encode;
use function mb_strlen;
use function method_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;
use function str_starts_with;
use function strpos;
use function substr;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * This document uses readOnly/writeOnly, which Symfony can only honour through serialization
 * groups — so every property here carries one. Pass the group, or a writeOnly field ends up in
 * the response:
 *   $serializer->normalize($dto, null, ['groups' => 'read']);
 *   $serializer->denormalize($payload, self::class, null, ['groups' => 'write']);
 */
final class ContainerAliases
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'properties' => [
            'listOfLists' => [
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'minLength' => 2,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'listOfMaps' => [
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'object',
                    'additionalProperties' => [
                        'type' => 'integer',
                        'minimum' => 0,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'mapOfLists' => [
                'additionalProperties' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'minLength' => 2,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'plain' => [
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'string',
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP = [
        'listOfLists' => 'listOfLists',
        'listOfMaps' => 'listOfMaps',
        'mapOfLists' => 'mapOfLists',
        'plain' => 'plain',
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS = [
        'listOfMaps' => 'listOfMapsProvided',
        'mapOfLists' => 'mapOfListsProvided',
        'plain' => 'plainProvided',
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * @var array<array<string>>
     */
    #[Groups(['read', 'write'])]
    private readonly array $listOfLists;

    /**
     * @var ?array<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    #[Groups(['read', 'write'])]
    #[Context(normalizationContext: [AbstractObjectNormalizer::SKIP_NULL_VALUES => true])]
    private ?array $listOfMaps = null;

    #[Ignore]
    private bool $listOfMapsProvided = false;

    /**
     * @var ?array<string, array<string>>
     */
    #[Groups(['read', 'write'])]
    #[Context(normalizationContext: [AbstractObjectNormalizer::SKIP_NULL_VALUES => true])]
    private ?array $mapOfLists = null;

    #[Ignore]
    private bool $mapOfListsProvided = false;

    /**
     * @var ?array<string>
     */
    #[Assert\All([new Assert\Length(min: 2)])]
    #[Groups(['read', 'write'])]
    #[Context(normalizationContext: [AbstractObjectNormalizer::SKIP_NULL_VALUES => true])]
    private ?array $plain = null;

    #[Ignore]
    private bool $plainProvided = false;

    /**
     * @param array<array<string>> $listOfLists
     */
    public function __construct(
        array $listOfLists,
    ) {
        $this->listOfLists = $listOfLists;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<array<string>>
     */
    public function getListOfLists(): array
    {
        return $this->listOfLists;
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getListOfMaps(): ?array
    {
        return $this->listOfMaps;
    }

    /**
     * @param ?array<array<string, mixed>> $listOfMaps
     */
    public function setListOfMaps(?array $listOfMaps): void
    {
        $this->listOfMaps = $listOfMaps;
        $this->listOfMapsProvided = true;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this key — true even when its value was null. Set by the setter,
     * so it answers for a request the serializer built and for a DTO assembled by hand alike.
     */
    #[Ignore]
    public function isListOfMapsProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->listOfMapsProvided;
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string, array<string>>
     */
    public function getMapOfLists(): ?array
    {
        return $this->mapOfLists;
    }

    /**
     * @param ?array<string, array<string>> $mapOfLists
     */
    public function setMapOfLists(?array $mapOfLists): void
    {
        $this->mapOfLists = $mapOfLists;
        $this->mapOfListsProvided = true;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this key — true even when its value was null. Set by the setter,
     * so it answers for a request the serializer built and for a DTO assembled by hand alike.
     */
    #[Ignore]
    public function isMapOfListsProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->mapOfListsProvided;
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string>
     */
    public function getPlain(): ?array
    {
        return $this->plain;
    }

    /**
     * @param ?array<string> $plain
     */
    public function setPlain(?array $plain): void
    {
        $this->plain = $plain;
        $this->plainProvided = true;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this key — true even when its value was null. Set by the setter,
     * so it answers for a request the serializer built and for a DTO assembled by hand alike.
     */
    #[Ignore]
    public function isPlainProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->plainProvided;
    }

    #[Assert\Callback]
    public function validateOpenApiConstraints(ExecutionContextInterface $context): void
    {
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $violation = $context->buildViolation(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            $path = self::openApiViolationPath($error);
            if ($path !== null) {
                $violation->atPath($path);
            }
            $violation->addViolation();
        }
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload — machinery for the generated validation, not an API to
     * call. It is public only because an enclosing DTO is a different class and needs this view of
     * a nested one. For output, use the Symfony serializer.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP as $phpName => $openApiName) {
            // A required property always exists. An optional one belongs in the payload only when
            // it was actually provided — its presence flag says so, which is also what keeps the
            // flags themselves out of the payload (iterating the object's properties would put
            // them in and trip `additionalProperties: false`).
            $flag = self::OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS[$phpName] ?? null;
            if ($flag !== null && $this->{$flag} !== true) {
                continue;
            }
            $payload[$openApiName] = $this->{$phpName};
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth > 0) {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        }
        try {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        } catch (\OverflowException $error) {
            return [$error->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        // An incomplete check is not a branch mismatch: not/if/anyOf must not invert or swallow it.
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            throw new \OverflowException($path . ' exceeds maximum validation depth.');
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = $typeConstraint === 'array' && is_array($normalizedValue)
                        ? sprintf('%s must be a JSON array (list with sequential keys), got an associative array', $path)
                        : sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if ((is_int($normalizedValue) || is_float($normalizedValue)) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minimum'])) {
            $minimum = $this->toNumericConstraint($schema['minimum'] ?? null);
            if ($minimum !== null && !($normalizedValue >= $minimum)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be greater than or equal to %s', $path, $this->stringifyOpenApiNumber($minimum));
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = $this->toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($propertyValue)) {
                        $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'array' => is_array($value) && array_is_list($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return get_object_vars($value);
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    /**
     * The property a message is about, as a value path, or null when it is about the payload itself.
     *
     * The interpreter bakes the subject INTO the sentence — `field "tags" must …`, and one level down
     * `field "tags".id must …` — because the wording has to match the runtime validator's word for
     * word. Reading it back here is what lets the violation carry the FIELD as well as the sentence:
     * an error response groups by property, and every keyword this interpreter owns was landing at
     * the root instead, where a per-field renderer never shows it.
     */
    private static function openApiViolationPath(string $error): ?string
    {
        if (!str_starts_with($error, 'field "')) {
            return null;
        }

        $closing = strpos($error, '"', 7);
        if ($closing === false) {
            return null;
        }

        $property = substr($error, 7, $closing - 7);
        if ($property === '') {
            return null;
        }

        // Anything after the closing quote up to the first space is the rest of the path, so
        // `field "tags".id must …` answers `tags.id` rather than just `tags`.
        $rest = substr($error, $closing + 1);
        $deeper = '';
        if (str_starts_with($rest, '.')) {
            $space = strpos($rest, ' ');
            $deeper = $space === false ? $rest : substr($rest, 0, $space);
        }

        return $property . $deeper;
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    private function toNumericConstraint(mixed $value): int|float|null
    {
        return is_int($value) || is_float($value) ? $value : null;
    }

    private function stringifyOpenApiNumber(int|float $value): string
    {
        $rendered = json_encode($value);
        return is_string($rendered) ? $rendered : (string)$value;
    }
}

    #[Assert\Callback]
    public function validateOpenApiConstraints(ExecutionContextInterface $context): void
    {
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $violation = $context->buildViolation(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            $path = self::openApiViolationPath($error);
            if ($path !== null) {
                $violation->atPath($path);
            }
            $violation->addViolation();
        }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload — machinery for the generated validation, not an API to
     * call. It is public only because an enclosing DTO is a different class and needs this view of
     * a nested one. For output, use the Symfony serializer.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_PHP_TO_NAME_MAP as $phpName => $openApiName) {
            // A required property always exists. An optional one belongs in the payload only when
            // it was actually provided — its presence flag says so, which is also what keeps the
            // flags themselves out of the payload (iterating the object's properties would put
            // them in and trip `additionalProperties: false`).
            $flag = self::OPENAPI_PROVIDED_FLAGS[$phpName] ?? null;
            if ($flag !== null && $this->{$flag} !== true) {
                continue;
            }
            $payload[$openApiName] = $this->{$phpName};
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth > 0) {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        }
        try {
            return $this->validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked($value, $schema, $path, $depth);
        } catch (\OverflowException $error) {
            return [$error->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNodeUnchecked(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        // An incomplete check is not a branch mismatch: not/if/anyOf must not invert or swallow it.
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            throw new \OverflowException($path . ' exceeds maximum validation depth.');
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiCallbackValue($value, $schema);
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['format'])) {
            if (is_string($format = $schema['format'] ?? null) && !$this->isValidOpenApiStringFormat($normalizedValue, $format)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must match format %s', $path, $format);
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($propertyValue)) {
                        $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'string' => is_string($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiCallbackValue(mixed $value, array $schema): mixed
    {
        if (!$value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            return $value;
        }

        $format = $schema['format'] ?? null;
        if (!is_string($format)) {
            return $value->format(DateTimeInterface::ATOM);
        }

        return match ($format) {
            'date' => $value->format('Y-m-d'),
            default => $value->format(DateTimeInterface::ATOM),
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return get_object_vars($value);
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    /**
     * The property a message is about, as a value path, or null when it is about the payload itself.
     *
     * The interpreter bakes the subject INTO the sentence — `field "tags" must …`, and one level down
     * `field "tags".id must …` — because the wording has to match the runtime validator's word for
     * word. Reading it back here is what lets the violation carry the FIELD as well as the sentence:
     * an error response groups by property, and every keyword this interpreter owns was landing at
     * the root instead, where a per-field renderer never shows it.
     */
    private static function openApiViolationPath(string $error): ?string
    {
        if (!str_starts_with($error, 'field "')) {
            return null;
        }

        $closing = strpos($error, '"', 7);
        if ($closing === false) {
            return null;
        }

        $property = substr($error, 7, $closing - 7);
        if ($property === '') {
            return null;
        }

        // Anything after the closing quote up to the first space is the rest of the path, so
        // `field "tags".id must …` answers `tags.id` rather than just `tags`.
        $rest = substr($error, $closing + 1);
        $deeper = '';
        if (str_starts_with($rest, '.')) {
            $space = strpos($rest, ' ');
            $deeper = $space === false ? $rest : substr($rest, 0, $space);
        }

        return $property . $deeper;
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function isValidOpenApiStringFormat(string $value, string $format): bool
    {
        return match ($format) {
            'date' => $this->isValidOpenApiDateFormat($value),
            'date-time' => $this->isValidOpenApiDateTimeFormat($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function isValidOpenApiDateFormat(string $value): bool
    {
        if (!preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $value)) {
            return false;
        }
        $date = DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $value);
        return $date !== false && $date->format('Y-m-d') === $value;
    }

    private function isValidOpenApiDateTimeFormat(string $value): bool
    {
        // The four shapes every mode agrees on, and a WARNING check per shape. `createFromFormat()`
        // alone is not a validator: it overflows silently, so `2026-13-45T99:99:99+00:00` parsed
        // clean and a payload the schema forbids was accepted. Only ATOM was tried before, which
        // also meant a sub-second value passed on leftover input rather than on being valid.
        foreach (['Y-m-d\TH:i:sp', 'Y-m-d\TH:i:s.up', 'Y-m-d H:i:s', 'Y-m-d\TH:i:s'] as $format) {
            if (DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat($format, $value) === false) {
                continue;
            }
            $errors = DateTimeImmutable::getLastErrors();
            if ($errors === false) {
                return true;
            }
            if ((int)($errors['warning_count'] ?? 0) === 0 && (int)($errors['error_count'] ?? 0) === 0) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
}
